# Searchbar

**Press Ctrl+F** (⌘F on a Mac) and type what you're looking for — a knob, a unit, a preset, a setting,
a function, an error word, *release*, *download*, *HardwareKit*… Every line is a link.

Each line reads: **name** · what it is · where it is — what it does.

Jump to: [GitHub pages](#github-pages) · [Units](#units) · [Presets](#presets) · [Docs](#docs) · [Parameters](#parameters) · [Glass-panel settings](#glass-panel-settings) · [Tests and tools](#tests-and-tools) · [Dev settings](#dev-settings) · [Code](#code) · [Files](#files)

*Made by [`scripts/make-searchbar.py`](scripts/make-searchbar.py) — run it again after changing docs or code.*

## Units

The ten units of the rack (and their old names), with their doc and their code. (22)

- **LEVEL CONTROL** · unit · [Devices/LEVEL CONTROL and OUTPUT MONITOR.md](Vault/Devices/LEVEL%20CONTROL%20and%20OUTPUT%20MONITOR.md) — how loud the rack runs
- **LEVEL CONTROL** · unit code · [Source/DSP/EnhEngine.cpp](Source/DSP/EnhEngine.cpp) — how loud the rack runs
- **ADAPTIVE ENHANCER** · unit (also: ENH MASTER unit, enhancer, black unit) · [Devices/ADAPTIVE ENHANCER.md](Vault/Devices/ADAPTIVE%20ENHANCER.md) — clarity, sub, footsteps
- **ADAPTIVE ENHANCER** · unit code · [Source/DSP/AdaptiveEQ.cpp](Source/DSP/AdaptiveEQ.cpp) — clarity, sub, footsteps
- **UPWARD LEVELER** · unit (also: LUMEN, leveler) · [Devices/UPWARD LEVELER.md](Vault/Devices/UPWARD%20LEVELER.md) — lifts quiet sounds
- **UPWARD LEVELER** · unit code · [Source/DSP/SpectralLeveler.cpp](Source/DSP/SpectralLeveler.cpp) — lifts quiet sounds
- **DEEP SUB** · unit (also: sub, hull, submarine) · [Devices/DEEP SUB.md](Vault/Devices/DEEP%20SUB.md) — octave-down sub and a ringing hull
- **DEEP SUB** · unit code · [Source/DSP/DeepSub.h](Source/DSP/DeepSub.h) — octave-down sub and a ringing hull
- **SPECTRAL LIMITER** · unit (also: anti-pumping, dynamic EQ) · [Devices/SPECTRAL LIMITER.md](Vault/Devices/SPECTRAL%20LIMITER.md) — takes bangs down where they are
- **SPECTRAL LIMITER** · unit code · [Source/DSP/SpectralLimiter.cpp](Source/DSP/SpectralLimiter.cpp) — takes bangs down where they are
- **MIX BALANCER** · unit (also: balancer, band faders) · [Devices/MIX BALANCER.md](Vault/Devices/MIX%20BALANCER.md) — keeps the mix in balance
- **MIX BALANCER** · unit code · [Source/DSP/MixBalancer.h](Source/DSP/MixBalancer.h) — keeps the mix in balance
- **ADAPTIVE COMPRESSOR** · unit (also: TIDE, compressor) · [Devices/ADAPTIVE COMPRESSOR.md](Vault/Devices/ADAPTIVE%20COMPRESSOR.md) — evens out the level
- **ADAPTIVE COMPRESSOR** · unit code · [Source/DSP/DynamicCompressor.cpp](Source/DSP/DynamicCompressor.cpp) — evens out the level
- **TONE & SPACE** · unit (also: SERAPH, SILK, HALO, HEAVEN, reverb, width) · [Devices/TONE and SPACE.md](Vault/Devices/TONE%20and%20SPACE.md) — polish, air, width, room
- **TONE & SPACE** · unit code · [Source/DSP/Seraph.cpp](Source/DSP/Seraph.cpp) — polish, air, width, room
- **CHARACTER** · unit (also: console, tape, valve, saturation, GRIT) · [Devices/CHARACTER.md](Vault/Devices/CHARACTER.md) — the sound of studio hardware
- **CHARACTER** · unit code · [Source/DSP/Character.h](Source/DSP/Character.h) — the sound of studio hardware
- **OUTPUT MONITOR** · unit (also: loudness meter, DUCK, COMPARE, A/B) · [Devices/LEVEL CONTROL and OUTPUT MONITOR.md](Vault/Devices/LEVEL%20CONTROL%20and%20OUTPUT%20MONITOR.md) — shows what the rack did
- **OUTPUT MONITOR** · unit code · [Source/DSP/LoudnessMeter.h](Source/DSP/LoudnessMeter.h) — shows what the rack did
- **Router app** · unit (also: standalone, gamers, INSERT RACK, VB-CABLE, tray) · [Tutorial/07 The router app.md](Vault/Tutorial/07%20The%20router%20app.md) — puts the rack between games and your headset
- **Router app** · code · [Source/Standalone/](Source/Standalone/AudioRouting.cpp) — the router: AudioRouting*, RouterBar, StandaloneApp

## Presets

Every factory preset, with its description. (13)

- **DEFAULT** · preset · [FactoryPresets.h:33](Source/Parameters/FactoryPresets.h#L33) — every unit at its default: a balanced starting point
- **COMPETITIVE FOOTSTEPS** · preset · [FactoryPresets.h:35](Source/Parameters/FactoryPresets.h#L35) — PvP: steps and detail forward, explosions do not duck them, no reverb smearing position
- **IMMERSIVE GAMES** · preset · [FactoryPresets.h:50](Source/Parameters/FactoryPresets.h#L50) — single-player and cinematic games: fuller low end, space and width, hits still controlled
- **NIGHT MODE** · preset · [FactoryPresets.h:66](Source/Parameters/FactoryPresets.h#L66) — quiet listening: small level range, quiet sounds lifted, sudden loud events held down hard
- **BASS HEAVY, PROTECTED** · preset · [FactoryPresets.h:78](Source/Parameters/FactoryPresets.h#L78) — big low end without the pumping: sub lift on, abnormal bass hits cut where they are
- **VOICE & STREAMING** · preset · [FactoryPresets.h:91](Source/Parameters/FactoryPresets.h#L91) — speech first: intelligibility, even level, no tail under the voice
- **MUSIC: WARM MASTER** · preset · [FactoryPresets.h:104](Source/Parameters/FactoryPresets.h#L104) — music: tape-like warmth and a touch of room, dynamics kept (no upward leveling)
- **MUSIC: WIDE & AIRY** · preset · [FactoryPresets.h:119](Source/Parameters/FactoryPresets.h#L119) — music: open top end, wide image, a lush modulated hall
- **DEEP SUB: SUBMARINE** · preset · [FactoryPresets.h:134](Source/Parameters/FactoryPresets.h#L134) — the low end deep, dark and huge: an octave-down sub under the bass and a steel hull ringing after every hit
- **MASTERING: ANALOG BUS** · preset · [FactoryPresets.h:150](Source/Parameters/FactoryPresets.h#L150) — a mix bus through iron and tape: a vintage console into 15 ips tape, gently driven, the rest of the rack light
- **GAME: ARENA** · preset · [FactoryPresets.h:162](Source/Parameters/FactoryPresets.h#L162) — footsteps and callouts forward, impact without mud: the game-tuned ARENA character on clean drive
- **TRANSPARENT (ALL OUT)** · preset · [FactoryPresets.h:170](Source/Parameters/FactoryPresets.h#L170) — a reference: every unit bypassed, the enhancer at STRENGTH 0 (its subsonic filter and the output safety limit…
- **Presets** · doc · [Presets.md](Vault/Reference/Presets.md) — what each preset is for, and editing presets.json

- **scene gaps** · AudioLab test scene · [AudioLab.cpp:215](Tools/AudioLab.cpp#L215) — music for 3 s, near silence for 3 s, music again: what the auto gains do in the gaps
- **scene bassduck** · AudioLab test scene · [AudioLab.cpp:216](Tools/AudioLab.cpp#L216) — unchanging quiet detail and footsteps throughout, loud bass only from 3 - 5 s: what the bass does to the rest
- **scene steps** · AudioLab test scene · [AudioLab.cpp:217](Tools/AudioLab.cpp#L217) — ambience and footsteps only, left/right, every 0.45 s: what the rack does to footsteps over time
- **scene game** · AudioLab test scene · [AudioLab.cpp:218](Tools/AudioLab.cpp#L218) — ambience, footsteps left/right, gunshots, an explosion, a voice
- **scene music** · AudioLab test scene · [AudioLab.cpp:219](Tools/AudioLab.cpp#L219) — drums, bass line, pad chord and lead at 120 bpm
- **scene drumsbass** · AudioLab test scene · [AudioLab.cpp:220](Tools/AudioLab.cpp#L220) — drums and a low bass line only (41 - 73 Hz)
- **scene bassline** · AudioLab test scene · [AudioLab.cpp:221](Tools/AudioLab.cpp#L221) — a sine bass line 41 - 110 Hz under a kick: the sub region
- **scene explosion** · AudioLab test scene · [AudioLab.cpp:222](Tools/AudioLab.cpp#L222) — steady 2 kHz detail and quiet steps, then a huge low explosion: ducking and pumping
- **scene quiet** · AudioLab test scene · [AudioLab.cpp:223](Tools/AudioLab.cpp#L223) — a -60 dBFS noise floor with quiet footsteps: upward levelling and the gate
- **scene sweep** · AudioLab test scene · [AudioLab.cpp:224](Tools/AudioLab.cpp#L224) — a -12 dBFS log sine sweep, 20 Hz - 20 kHz: response, harmonics, aliasing
- **scene tones** · AudioLab test scene · [AudioLab.cpp:225](Tools/AudioLab.cpp#L225) — 1 kHz, 100 Hz and 40 Hz at -6 dBFS, then 60 Hz + 7 kHz (4:1): THD and IMD
- **scene impulses** · AudioLab test scene · [AudioLab.cpp:226](Tools/AudioLab.cpp#L226) — short 2 kHz bursts every 0.5 s over silence: transients, ringing, pre-echo
- **scene pink** · AudioLab test scene · [AudioLab.cpp:227](Tools/AudioLab.cpp#L227) — -18 dBFS pink noise: the rack's static tone
- **scene voice** · AudioLab test scene · [AudioLab.cpp:228](Tools/AudioLab.cpp#L228) — a voice over a low bed: intelligibility, the leveler
- **scene start** · AudioLab test scene · [AudioLab.cpp:229](Tools/AudioLab.cpp#L229) — 1.5 s of digital silence, then steady wide pink noise at -20 dBFS: does anything surge when sound starts
- **scene wide** · AudioLab test scene · [AudioLab.cpp:230](Tools/AudioLab.cpp#L230) — a wide ambience (unrelated left and right) with quiet footsteps: is wide material treated as mono would be
- **scene antiphase** · AudioLab test scene · [AudioLab.cpp:231](Tools/AudioLab.cpp#L231) — footsteps and ambience with the right channel upside down: out-of-phase material
- **scene silence** · AudioLab test scene · [AudioLab.cpp:232](Tools/AudioLab.cpp#L232) — music for 2 s, then digital silence: does the output fall silent (no hiss, no endless tails, no drift)
